Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The wave variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 651 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 664 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 651 + O(1/n^664). Summing over all modes and applying the wave convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
